                                                            │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                                                                                                     │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.

CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                                    ENV CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                                                                                                     │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.              結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.             │                                                                               │    Use:                                                                       │                                                                               │    - docker compose env_file: for local development convenience                Do not copy .env into images. CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                                    ENV CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                                                                                                     │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.              結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.             │                                                                               │    Use:                                                                       │                                                                               │    - docker compose env_file: for local development convenience                Do not copy .env into images. CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                                    ENV CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                                                                                                     │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.              結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.             │                                                                               │    Use:                                                                       │                                                                               │    - docker compose env_file: for local development convenience                Do not copy .env into images. CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                                    ENV CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                                                                                                     │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.              結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.             │                                                                               │    Use:                                                                       │                                                                               │    - docker compose env_file: for local development convenience                Do not copy .env into images. CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                                    ENV CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                                                                                                     │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.              結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.             │                                                                               │    Use:                                                                       │                                                                               │    - docker compose env_file: for local development convenience                Do not copy .env into images. CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                                    ENV CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                                                                                                     │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.              結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.             │                                                                               │    Use:                                                                       │                                                                               │    - docker compose env_file: for local development convenience                Do not copy .env into images. CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                                    ENV CONFIG_VERSION=${CONFIG_VERSION}                                   │                                                                               │                                                                               │                                                                               │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                                                                                                     │    --------                                                                   │                                                                               │    結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.              結論（Conclusion）                                                         │                                                                               │    Do not copy .env into images. This violates the principle of least         │    privilege and creates persistent, unrotatable secret exposure.             │                                                                               │    Use:                                                                       │                                                                               │    - docker compose env_file: for local development convenience                Do not copy .env into images.
